## Required endpoint gates

An early YES needs the complete canonical full-plane problem, exact candidate, target spaces including center factorization and infinity, validated forcing norm, bounded inverse or legitimate alternative, nonlinear estimate, normalization, and strict closing inequality. A NO needs an audited exclusion in a quantified nontrivial uncertainty neighborhood. Failure of one estimate or a nonzero trial residual does not pass either gate.

## 81. Radial ODE defect criterion, 2026-10-21T01:03:59.681497+00:00

CONFIRMED abstract criterion and illustrative moment arithmetic. See research/radial_defect/PROOF.md and ADDENDUM.md. In canonical conventions, P=l*psi/r² and R=psi_r/r satisfy P'=-2P+lR and R'=lP-2R-f. Hence qplus=P+R solves qplus'=(l-2)qplus-f; qminus=P-R solves qminus'=-(l+2)qminus+f. The forcing exponent is alpha+2; alpha+4 belongs to the unscaled Green-moment equations. The zero growth rate at l=2 is covered.

For q'=lambda*q+g and a candidate polynomial p, the error is bounded by its initial anchor error times exp(lambda*t), plus the positive convolution moments of the polynomial defect envelope. The proof gives explicit monotone moments, a signed exponential-series enclosure with exact rational geometric tail, and a sufficient uniform test against the candidate Taylor-model radius. This preserves the anchor uncertainty.

Independent checks:20 symbolic identities;100 illustrative moments;240 primary Arb512/iv180 enclosures and400 inequalities. A fresh exact-rational implementation, with no producer/numpy/Arb/mpmath imports, checks100 moments,98 geometric tails and120 full containments inside the primary hulls. These are consistency checks of the illustrative values, not an audit of actual flow data. Actual Opus5.5 reviewed the proof and the complete closure bundle; final review accepted at01:01:18Z, request45de8e56-5b06-4d0b-a2af-fa78e29370e3. Verdict 23pins SHAce3a83f721fd2817f11c9330e1ac8b88d053ed289fe2d21e4225e85cc84b7008.

||Dw||F, so it requires a new estimate.

## 187 FINAL, 2026-10-22T02:23:52.657393+00:00: quasimodes for every fixed higher center weight

Verdict CONFIRMED for the exactly normalized canonical modal trial. For each fixed integer m>=2, take the weighted weak-H1 norm with w_m=L^(2m+2) r^(-2m-2)(1+r^2/L^2)^(m-k-1/2).
